Equation 1: x^2-4x-3 = 0
Solution:
coefficients: a = 1, b = -4, c = -3
Discriminant D = b * b - 4 * a * c = -4 * -4 - 4 * 1 * -3 = 28
D > 0, two roots:
x1 = (-b + sqrt(D))/(2 * a) = (4 + sqrt(28))/(2 * 1)
x2 = (-b - sqrt(D))/(2 * a) = (4 - sqrt(28))/(2 * 1)
Roots: 4.64575, -0.645751

Equation 2: x^2+9x = 0
Solution:
coefficients: a = 1, b = 9, c = 0
Discriminant D = b * b - 4 * a * c = 9 * 9 - 4 * 1 * 0 = 81
D > 0, two roots:
x1 = (-b + sqrt(D))/(2 * a) = (-9 + sqrt(81))/(2 * 1)
x2 = (-b - sqrt(D))/(2 * a) = (-9 - sqrt(81))/(2 * 1)
Roots: 0, -9

x1 = (-b + sqrt(D))/(2 * a) = (-8 + sqrt(260))/(2 * 7)
x2 = (-b - sqrt(D))/(2 * a) = (-8 - sqrt(260))/(2 * 7)
Roots: 0.580323, -1.72318
